		id = USA_prepare_for_the_1956_elections

		y = 7
		cost = 1.5

		allow_branch = {
			OR = {
				tag = UOA
				
				AND = {
					NOT = { tag = NSA }
					NOT = { tag = CCA }
					
					NOT = {
						has_global_flag = second_american_civil_war
						OR = {
							is_nationalist = yes
							is_communist = yes
							is_left_wing_radical = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		available = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = american_elections_starting
				date > 1956.7.9
				date < 1956.11.6
			}
		}

		completion_reward = {
			add_political_power = 25
		}
	}
